# Skills · D · part 139

802 entries, dsf-first-derivative-curve-peak-detection to dsql. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **dsf-first-derivative-curve-peak-detection**: Use when classifying DSF melting curves via peaks in normalized, smoothed first derivative curves (FDCs): qualifying peaks must exceed 10% of the highest peak's width and height; flag no-Tm curves (&gt;=4 peaks) and unfolded curves (declining MC). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/dsf-first-derivative-curve-peak-detection))
- **dsf-fluorescence-data-processing**: Use when normalized DSF melting curves contain fluorescence noise that would distort melting temperature (Tm) estimation, and a shape-preserving smoothing step is needed before Tm analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/dsf-fluorescence-data-processing))
- **dsf-gan-utility-eval**: Evaluates the predictive utility of synthetic tabular data generated by a GAN. It measures how well a downstream classifier or regressor trained on the synthetic samples performs when evaluated on a strictly held-out real validation set. Use when the user wants to benchmark on Two distinct tabular datasets (names in Appendix A), or asks about evaluating this task. Reports model performance. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/dsf-gan-utility-eval) · ★3)
- **dsf-melting-curve-processing**: Use when raw DSF fluorescence-versus-temperature melting curves (e.g. a 96-well plate) must be normalized, smoothed, and converted to per-well Tm values, or when checking whether smoothing perturbs FD-method Tm values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/dsf-melting-curve-processing))
- **dsf-melting-curve-smoothing-comparison**: Use when you have raw DSF melting curves plus SimpleDSFviewer-smoothed counterparts (e.g. His-FGF7 + 50 µM heparin exemplar) and must verify smoothing preserves MC shape via per-point difference analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/dsf-melting-curve-smoothing-comparison))
- **dsf-thermal-stability-analysis**: Use when the input is DSF fluorescence melting-curve data (e.g. a 96-well exemplar plate ramp) and the goal is to compute normalized FD curves, extract per-well Tm values, or compare Tm from smoothed vs unsmoothed MCs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/dsf-thermal-stability-analysis))

- **dsh-video-still-first**: AI 短片的"静帧先行"管线与跨镜头一致性：先用文生图/图生图定角色与场景锚点，再用静帧当首帧跑 i2v。含 image_edit 的硬规则（大改动有效、微调退化，移除指令必须逐项点名）、编辑会带进时代不对的道具、**模型会凭空加"不舒服的东西"（城门挂人、刑具、尸体），负面词要预先点名**、**首帧姿势决定它能不能动（抬手会让手臂凭空消失）**、**静帧还要过"动不动"这关——整齐方阵/背影海/平铺纹理一动就假**、**i2v 不是动作生成器：人群冲击该换 t2v**、配角脸撞主角会让观众认错人（群戏一律背对镜头）、静帧必须与运动提示词对齐否则一开场就跳、LoadImage 缓存让新静帧不生效、以及交付前"每镜抽 5 帧横条肉眼扫一遍"的核对法。要做有多镜头、有固定角色的 AI 短片时加载。 ([zjwan461/dsh-docker](https://github.com/zjwan461/dsh-docker/tree/HEAD/skills/dsh-video-still-first) · ★1)
